  %select_n.518 = f64[] select(%eq.27, %constant.592, %param_1.9), metadata={op_name="select_n" stack_frame_id=330}

  %constant.590 = s64[] constant(9223372036854775807)
  %xor.30 = s64[] xor(%constant.590, %bitcast-convert.30)
  %select.44 = s64[] select(%compare.34, %xor.30, %bitcast-convert.30)
